--- Commit Message ---
LU-19722 llite: send NO_APPEND flag from client

If the client sends the OBD_CONNECT2_NO_APPEND flag, it indicates
that it properly handles file extent locking when open(O_APPEND) is
used, so the MDS does not need to constrict PFL file layouts with
the 'append_stripe_count' and 'append_pool' parameters.

That allows new clients to use normal PFL or other composite layouts
without inadvertently instantiating all of the (widely-striped) PFL
components of a file, while avoiding old clients doing the same.

This is the client half of the patch to enable this feature.  Add a
client-side 'append_layout' mount option to re-enable the
append-specific layout by not sending the NO_APPEND flag to the MDS at
mount, in case it is needed for some reason, default to off.  The
expectation is that users/administrators prefer using normal PFL
layouts to handle O_APPEND files as with other files, rather than a
single global layout, now that append-specific layouts aren't needed.

The OBD_CONNECT2_NO_APPEND flag can eventually be ignored by the MDS,
and the 'append_stripe_count' and 'append_pool' parameters removed,
once all clients include https://review.whamcloud.com/69187
("LU-19900 lov: split DIO appends at stripe boundaries"),
likely 2.18.0.

Create sanity-flr.sh test_72b(), which is similar to the renamed
test_72() (now test_72a), but inherits a PFL layout from its parent
and is created with open(O_APPEND) instead of setstripe.

Since the MDS no longer applies the 'append_*' parameters to a
client that sends NO_APPEND, sanity.sh test_27M and test_27U
remount the client with 'append_layout' so that they keep
exercising those parameters.  Dump the file layout in test_131b
if the append writev check fails.

Also fix sanity-flr test_0a for 2.17 servers, which leave the mirror
count limit to lfs, so only expect too many mirrors to fail with a
newer MDS.  Restart the OSTs stopped by test_34a and test_34b with
stack_trap, so that a failure there does not leave them stopped and
hang test_35 until the session times out.

Test-Parameters: testlist=sanity-dom env=ONLY=sanity
Test-Parameters: testlist=sanity serverversion=2.17 env=SKIP27D="-s 37"
Test-Parameters: testlist=sanity-flr serverversion=2.17
Fixes: 0f21e02f17 ("LU-12738 pfl: do not instantiate full PFL layout on append")
Fixes: e2ac6e1eaa ("LU-9341 lod: Add special O_APPEND striping")

--- Message (Gerrit AI review for Lustre, 2026-07-29 12:08:15.000000000) ---
Patch Set 1:

(5 comments)

AI code review results below, please doublecheck

Some concerns, see inline comments - test_72b() looks like it will fail as written.

It is also worth double-checking that the server half actually consumes this flag on the path that matters. sp_append is only set in mdt_create_unpack() (REINT_CREATE), but MDS_OPEN_APPEND only reaches the MDS through mdt_open_unpack() (REINT_OPEN), and sp_append is not cleared in mdt_thread_info_reset(). For open(O_CREAT|O_APPEND) mdd_object_make_hint() therefore reads whatever sp_append was left behind by an earlier request on the same service thread, so OBD_CONNECT2_NO_APPEND has no deterministic effect - and test_72b() cannot tell a client that sends the flag from one that does not.

AI code review results below, please doublecheck

Some concerns, see inline comments. On a stock filesystem mdd_append_stripe_count defaults to 1 (mdd_device.c:173), so enabling this flag changes the layout of every O_APPEND-created file, and sanity.sh test_27M/test_27U still assert the old behaviour behind MDS-only version gates. Should this series update that coverage as well?

--- Message (Gerrit AI review for Lustre, 2026-09-18 05:21:15.000000000) ---
Patch Set 13:

(2 comments)

AI code review results below, please doublecheck

Some concerns, see inline comments.

The main one is not in the diff itself: flipping the default makes every O_APPEND-created file inherit the parent's composite layout, and that routes append writes through a lov/direct-IO path that never chunks at a component or stripe boundary. The test_131b failures mentioned in the commit message look like that bug surfacing rather than a test problem, so it seems worth fixing the append DIO chunking before the client starts advertising NO_APPEND by default.
